import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
import { Vazirmatn, Playfair_Display } from "next/font/google";
import { Toaster } from "sonner";
import "./globals.css";

const vazirmatn = Vazirmatn({ subsets: ["arabic", "latin"], variable: "--font-vazirmatn", display: "swap" });
const playfair = Playfair_Display({ subsets: ["latin"], variable: "--font-playfair", display: "swap" });

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: { default: "فاطمه لب | زیبایی و مراقبت پوست", template: "%s | فاطمه لب" },
  description: "در فاطمه‌لب، محصولات آرایشی و مراقبتی را با اطلاعات روشن و تجربه‌ای ساده برای انتخاب بررسی کنید.",
  metadataBase: new URL(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL || "https://fatemehlab.ir"),
};

export const viewport: Viewport = { themeColor: "#FDFBF7", width: "device-width", initialScale: 1 };

export default function RootLayout({ children }: Readonly<{ children: React.ReactNode }>) {
  return (
    <html lang="fa" dir="rtl" className={`${vazirmatn.variable} ${playfair.variable}`}>
      <body className="font-sans min-h-screen bg-canvas noise-overlay">
        <Toaster position="top-center" richColors theme="light" />
        {children}
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Camera, Mail, Phone, MapPin, Send } from 'lucide-react';

export default function Footer() {
  // مقدار اولیه خالی است تا در زمان بیلد، Next.js با خطا مواجه نشود
  const [currentYear, setCurrentYear] = useState('');

  // این بخش فقط بعد از باز شدن سایت در مرورگر کاربر اجرا می‌شود
  useEffect(() => {
    const year = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fa-IR', {
      year: 'numeric',
    }).format(new Date());
    setCurrentYear(year);
  }, []);

  return (
    <footer className="bg-beige border-t border-border mt-24">
      <div className="container-custom py-16 border-b border-border">

              از جدیدترین محصولات، تخفیف‌های ویژه و مقالات آموزشی باخبر شوید.
            </p>
          </div>
          <form className="flex gap-2">
            <input
              type="email"
              placeholder="ایمیل شما"
              className="flex-1 px-4 py-3.5 bg-background border border-border text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-sage transition-colors"

'use client';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { Search, ShoppingBag, Menu, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useCartStore } from '@/store/cart';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export default function Header() {
  const items = useCartStore((state) => state.items);
  const [isMounted, setIsMounted] = useState(false);
  const [isMobileMenuOpen, setIsMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => { setIsMounted(true); }, []);
  const cartCount = isMounted ? items.reduce((total, item) => total + item.quantity, 0) : 0;

  return (
    <>
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 glass-panel-strong border-b border-white/40">
        <div className="container-custom h-[72px] flex items-center justify-between gap-6">
          <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
            <div className="relative w-9 h-9 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-white/60 shadow-sm">
              <Image src="/logo.png" alt="فاطمه لب" fill className="object-cover" priority />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col">
              <span className="text-base font-bold tracking-tight text-ink leading-none">فاطمه لب</span>
              <span className="text-[8px] tracking-[0.35em] text-muted mt-1">FATEMEHLAB</span>
            </div>
          </Link>

          <nav className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-9">
            {[
              { href: '/products', label: 'محصولات' },
              { href: '/about', label: 'درباره ما' },
              { href: '/track', label: 'رهگیری سفارش' },
              { href: '/faq', label: 'سوالات متداول' },
            ].map((link) => (
              <Link key={link.href} href={link.href} className="text-[13px] font-medium text-muted hover:text-ink transition-colors duration-200 relative group">
                {link.label}
                <span className="absolute -bottom-1 right-0 w-0 h-[1px] bg-sage group-hover:w-full transition-all duration-300" />
              </Link>
            ))}
          </nav>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button className="p-2.5 text-muted hover:text-ink transition-colors"><Search className="w-[18px] h-[18px]" /></button>
            <Link href="/cart" className="relative p-2.5 text-muted hover:text-ink transition-colors">
              <ShoppingBag className="w-[18px] h-[18px]" />
              {cartCount > 0 && (
                <span className="absolute top-1 right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-sage text-white text-[9px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center rounded-full shadow-sm">{cartCount}</span>
              )}
            </Link>
            <button className="md:hidden p-2.5 text-muted" onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(true)}><Menu className="w-5 h-5" /></button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {isMobileMenuOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] md:hidden bg-canvas/95 backdrop-blur-2xl">
          <div className="flex flex-col h-full">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-5 border-b border-line/60">
              <span className="font-bold">منو</span>
              <button onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)} className="p-2 -ml-2"><X className="w-5 h-5" /></button>
            </div>
            <nav className="flex-1 p-6 space-y-1">
              {[
                { href: '/products', label: 'محصولات' },
                { href: '/about', label: 'درباره ما' },
                { href: '/track', label: 'رهگیری سفارش' },
                { href: '/faq', label: 'سوالات متداول' },
              ].map((link) => (
                <Link key={link.href} href={link.href} onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)} className="block py-4 text-lg font-medium border-b border-line/60 text-ink hover:text-sage transition-colors">
                  {link.label}
                </Link>
              ))}
            </nav>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src/db/schema.ts

import { pgTable, text, timestamp, integer, boolean, uuid, jsonb } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

export const categories = pgTable("categories", {
  id: uuid("id").defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  name: text("name").notNull(),
  slug: text("slug").notNull().unique(),
  sku: text("sku").unique(),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const products = pgTable("products", {
  id: uuid("id").defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  categoryId: uuid("category_id").references(() => categories.id),
  name: text("name").notNull(),
  slug: text("slug").notNull().unique(),
  sku: text("sku").unique(),
  description: text("description"),
  price: integer("price").notNull().default(0),
  compareAtPrice: integer("compare_at_price"), 
  stock: integer("stock").notNull().default(0),
  status: text("status").notNull().default("draft"),
  isFeatured: boolean("is_featured").notNull().default(false),
  mainImage: text("main_image"),
  gallery: jsonb("gallery").$type<string[]>().default([]),
  specifications: jsonb("specifications").$type<{key: string, value: string}[]>().default([]),
  ingredients: text("ingredients"),
  usage: text("usage"),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const adminUsers = pgTable("admin_users", {
  id: uuid("id").defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  email: text("email").notNull().unique(),
  passwordHash: text("password_hash").notNull(),
  role: text("role").notNull().default("admin"),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const siteSettings = pgTable("site_settings", {
  key: text("key").primaryKey(),
  value: jsonb("value").notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
});
export const orders = pgTable("orders", {
  id: uuid("id").defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  firstName: text("first_name").notNull(),
  lastName: text("last_name").notNull(),
  phone: text("phone").notNull(),
  address: text("address").notNull(),
  city: text("city").notNull(),
  postalCode: text("postal_code").notNull(),
  notes: text("notes"),
  totalAmount: integer("total_amount").notNull(),
  status: text("status").notNull().default("pending"), // pending, paid, processing, shipped, delivered, cancelled
  trackingCode: text("tracking_code").notNull().unique().default("FL-PENDING"),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const orderItems = pgTable("order_items", {
  id: uuid("id").defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  orderId: uuid("order_id").references(() => orders.id).notNull(),
  productId: uuid("product_id").references(() => products.id).notNull(),
  productName: text("product_name").notNull(),
  price: integer("price").notNull(),
  quantity: integer("quantity").notNull(),
});
export const articles = pgTable("articles", {
  id: uuid("id").defaultRandom().primaryKey(),
  title: text("title").notNull(),
  slug: text("slug").notNull().unique(),
  excerpt: text("excerpt"),
  content: text("content").notNull(),
  category: text("category").notNull().default("education"),
  coverImage: text("cover_image"),
  status: text("status").notNull().default("draft"),
  readTime: text("read_time").default("۵ دقیقه"),
  author: text("author").default("تیم فاطمه‌لب"),
  featured: boolean("featured").notNull().default(false),
  editorsPick: boolean("editors_pick").notNull().default(false),
  seoTitle: text("seo_title"),
  seoDescription: text("seo_description"),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
});

FILE: src/app/products/page.tsx
==================================================
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { categories, products } from '@/db/schema';
import { and, desc, eq, ilike, SQL } from 'drizzle-orm';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { ArrowLeft, Search, SlidersHorizontal, PackageSearch } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatToman } from '@/lib/currency';
import { getPublicProductDescription, getPublicProductImage } from '@/lib/product-content';
import Header from '@/components/storefront/Header';
import Footer from '@/components/storefront/Footer';

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: 'محصولات',
  description: 'محصولات آرایشی و مراقبتی فاطمه‌لب را بررسی کنید و مشخصات، قیمت و وضعیت موجودی هر محصول را ببینید.',
};

export const instant = false;

export default async function ProductsPage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: Promise<{ q?: string; category?: string }> }) {
  const { q = '', category = '' } = await searchParams;
  const searchTerm = q.trim();

  const categoryRows = await db.select().from(categories).orderBy(categories.name);
  const selectedCategory = categoryRows.find((item) => item.slug === category);
  const invalidCategory = Boolean(category && !selectedCategory);

  const conditions: SQL[] = [eq(products.status, 'published')];
  if (searchTerm) conditions.push(ilike(products.name, `%${searchTerm}%`));
  if (selectedCategory) conditions.push(eq(products.categoryId, selectedCategory.id));

  const dbProducts = invalidCategory ? [] : await db.select().from(products).where(and(...conditions)).orderBy(desc(products.createdAt));

  const buildCategoryHref = (slug?: string) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    if (searchTerm) params.set('q', searchTerm);
    if (slug) params.set('category', slug);
    const query = params.toString();
    return query ? `/products?${query}` : '/products';
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-background">
      <Header />
      <main className="flex-1">
        <section className="fl-page-intro">
          <div className="container-custom">
            <span className="fl-eyebrow"><span className="fl-eyebrow-dot" />FATEMEHLAB / PRODUCT CATALOG</span>
            <h1 className="fl-page-title">محصولات فاطمه‌لب</h1>
            <p className="fl-page-description">محصول موردنظرتان را پیدا کنید و برای مشاهده تصاویر، مشخصات ثبت‌شده، قیمت و وضعیت موجودی وارد صفحه آن شوید.</p>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="container-custom py-6 border-b border-[#ece8ed]">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
            <form action="/products" method="get" className="relative w-full lg:max-w-[370px]">
              <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#99919d]" size={17} />
              <input type="search" name="q" defaultValue={searchTerm} placeholder="نام محصول را جست‌وجو کنید..." className="fl-input pr-10 pl-4" aria-label="جست‌وجوی محصولات" />
              {category && <input type="hidden" name="category" value={category} />}
            </form>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2" aria-label="فیلتر دسته‌بندی">
              <span className="ml-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#77717b]"><SlidersHorizontal size={14} /> دسته‌بندی</span>
              <Link href={buildCategoryHref()} className={`fl-filter-chip ${!category ? 'is-active' : ''}`}>همه محصولات</Link>
              {categoryRows.map((item) => (
                <Link key={item.id} href={buildCategoryHref(item.slug)} className={`fl-filter-chip ${category === item.slug ? 'is-active' : ''}`}>{item.name}</Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="container-custom py-9 md:py-12">
          <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <p className="text-[11px] text-[#817984]">
              {dbProducts.length.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} محصول
              {searchTerm ? ` برای «${searchTerm}»` : ''}
              {selectedCategory ? ` در دسته ${selectedCategory.name}` : ''}
            </p>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-[#a49ca7]">نمایش اطلاعات ثبت‌شده فروشگاه</span>
          </div>

          {dbProducts.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="fl-empty-state">
              <div className="mx-auto mb-4 grid h-12 w-12 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-[#fff0f7] text-[#c61d70]"><PackageSearch size={23} /></div>
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b]">محصولی پیدا نشد</h2>
              <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-sm text-xs leading-7 text-[#77717b]">عبارت جست‌وجو یا دسته‌بندی را تغییر دهید تا محصولات مرتبط را ببینید.</p>
              <Link href="/products" className="btn-primary mt-5">نمایش همه محصولات <ArrowLeft size={15} /></Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="fl-product-grid">
              {dbProducts.map((product) => {
                const hasPrice = product.price > 0;
                const isAvailable = product.stock > 0 && hasPrice;
                const productImage = getPublicProductImage(product.mainImage);
                const productDescription = getPublicProductDescription(product.description);
                return (
                  <Link key={product.id} href={`/products/${product.slug}`} className="fl-product-card group">
                    <div className="fl-product-media">
                      {productImage ? (
                        <Image src={productImage} alt={`تصویر ${product.name}`} fill unoptimized={productImage.startsWith('http')} sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, (max-width: 1024px) 50vw, 25vw" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="fl-product-placeholder" aria-hidden="true"><span className="absolute text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[.16em] text-[#b77b9a]">FATEMEHLAB</span></div>
                      )}
                      {!isAvailable && (
                        <span className="absolute right-3 top-3 rounded-lg border border-white/70 bg-white/90 px-2.5 py-1 text-[9px] font-bold text-[#8a6478]">{hasPrice ? 'ناموجود' : 'قیمت ثبت نشده'}</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="fl-product-info">
                      <h2 className="fl-product-name">{product.name}</h2>
                      <p className="fl-product-desc">{productDescription || 'مشاهده مشخصات و اطلاعات ثبت‌شده'}</p>
                      <div className="fl-product-bottom">
                        <span className="fl-product-price">{hasPrice ? formatToman(product.price) : 'قیمت ثبت نشده'}</span>
                        <span className={`fl-product-status ${isAvailable ? '' : 'is-out'}`}>{isAvailable ? 'موجود' : hasPrice ? 'ناموجود' : 'در انتظار قیمت'}</span>
                      </div>
                      <span className="fl-product-view">مشاهده جزئیات <ArrowLeft size={13} /></span>
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}

==================================================
FILE: src/app/products/[slug]/page.tsx
==================================================
import type { Metadata } from 'next';
import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { and, eq, ne } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowUpLeft, ChevronLeft, ClipboardList, Droplets, Package, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { products } from '@/db/schema';
import { formatToman } from '@/lib/currency';
import { getPublicProductDescription, getPublicProductImage } from '@/lib/product-content';
import Header from '@/components/storefront/Header';
import Footer from '@/components/storefront/Footer';
import ProductGallery from '@/components/product/ProductGallery';
import AddToCartButton from './add-to-cart';

export const instant = false;

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const [product] = await db.select().from(products).where(eq(products.slug, slug)).limit(1);
  if (!product || product.status !== 'published') return { title: 'محصول یافت نشد' };
  return {
    title: product.name,
    description: product.description || `مشاهده مشخصات و اطلاعات ${product.name} در فاطمه‌لب.`,
  };
}

export default async function ProductDetailPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
  const { slug } = await params;
  let productData;
  try {
    productData = await db.select().from(products).where(eq(products.slug, slug)).limit(1);
  } catch {
    notFound();
  }

  const product = productData[0];
  if (!product || product.status !== 'published') notFound();

  // منطق سخت‌گیرانه: قیمت باید بیشتر از صفر باشد تا محصول "موجود" در نظر گرفته شود
  const isPriced = product.price > 0;
  const isAvailable = product.stock > 0 && isPriced; 
  const publicDescription = getPublicProductDescription(product.description);
  const publicMainImage = getPublicProductImage(product.mainImage);
  const publicGallery = (product.gallery || []).map((image) => getPublicProductImage(image)).filter((image): image is string => Boolean(image));

  const relatedProducts = await db.select().from(products).where(and(eq(products.status, 'published'), ne(products.id, product.id))).limit(4);

  const specifications = Array.isArray(product.specifications) ? product.specifications : [];
  const hasAdditionalDetails = specifications.length > 0 || Boolean(product.ingredients) || Boolean(product.usage);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-background">
      <Header />
      <main className="flex-1 pb-24 md:pb-0">
        <div className="container-custom py-5">
          <nav className="fl-breadcrumb" aria-label="مسیر صفحه">
            <Link href="/">صفحه اصلی</Link>
            <ChevronLeft size={13} />
            <Link href="/products">محصولات</Link>
            <ChevronLeft size={13} />
            <span className="font-semibold text-[#514a55]">{product.name}</span>
          </nav>
        </div>

        <section className="container-custom pb-10 md:pb-14">
          <div className="fl-detail-grid">
            <div className="lg:sticky lg:top-24 lg:self-start">
              <ProductGallery mainImage={publicMainImage} gallery={publicGallery} name={product.name} />
            </div>
            <div className="pt-1">
              <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="rounded-lg bg-[#fff0f7] px-3 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-[#b31a65]">اطلاعات محصول</span>
                {product.sku && <span className="text-[10px] text-[#918895]" dir="ltr">SKU: {product.sku}</span>}
              </div>
              <h1 className="fl-detail-title">{product.name}</h1>
              {publicDescription ? <p className="fl-detail-description">{publicDescription}</p> : <p className="fl-detail-description">اطلاعات تکمیلی این محصول پس از ثبت مشخصات تأییدشده نمایش داده می‌شود.</p>}

              <div className="fl-price-box mt-6 space-y-3">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                  {isPriced ? (
                    <>
                      <span className="fl-price">{formatToman(product.price)}</span>
                      {product.compareAtPrice && product.compareAtPrice > product.price && (
                        <span className="text-sm text-[#9a929d] line-through">{formatToman(product.compareAtPrice)}</span>
                      )}
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="text-sm font-bold text-[#8a697b]">به زودی عرضه می‌شود</span>
                  )}
                </div>
                
                {/* اصلاح مهم: اگر قیمت ثبت نشده باشد، موجودی نمایش داده نمی‌شود */}
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  {isPriced && (
                    <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold ${isAvailable ? 'bg-[#edf8f2] text-[#247b53]' : 'bg-[#f7f2f5] text-[#88717e]'}`}>
                      <span className={`h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full ${isAvailable ? 'bg-[#279866]' : 'bg-[#aa8e9d]'}`} />
                      {isAvailable ? 'موجود' : 'ناموجود'}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 hidden max-w-md md:block"><AddToCartButton product={product} /></div>

              <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#eee9ef] bg-white p-3 text-[10px] text-[#706976]"><ClipboardList size={16} className="shrink-0 text-[#c61d70]" />مشخصات محصول</div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#eee9ef] bg-white p-3 text-[10px] text-[#706976]"><Package size={16} className="shrink-0 text-[#c61d70]" />وضعیت موجودی</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="container-custom pb-12 md:pb-16">
          <div className="mb-5"><span className="fl-section-kicker">اطلاعات محصول</span><h2 className="fl-section-title">جزئیات محصول</h2></div>
          {hasAdditionalDetails ? (
            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              {specifications.length > 0 && (
                <div className="fl-detail-section">
                  <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-[#eee9ef] pb-3"><Package size={17} className="text-[#c61d70]" /><h3 className="text-[13px] font-bold text-[#302a33]">مشخصات محصول</h3></div>
                  {specifications.map((spec, index) => (
                    <div className="fl-spec-row" key={`${spec.key}-${index}`}>
                      <span className="text-[#817985]">{spec.key}</span>
                      <span className="max-w-[65%] text-left font-semibold text-[#37313a]" dir="auto">{spec.value}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
              {product.ingredients && (
                <div className="fl-detail-section">
                  <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-[#eee9ef] pb-3"><Sparkles size={17} className="text-[#c61d70]" /><h3 className="text-[13px] font-bold text-[#302a33]">ترکیبات ثبت‌شده</h3></div>
                  <p className="text-[12px] leading-[2.1] text-[#706976]">{product.ingredients}</p>
                </div>
              )}
              {product.usage && (
                <div className="fl-detail-section">
                  <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-[#eee9ef] pb-3"><Droplets size={17} className="text-[#c61d70]" /><h3 className="text-[13px] font-bold text-[#302a33]">روش استفاده</h3></div>
                  <p className="text-[12px] leading-[2.1] text-[#706976]">{product.usage}</p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="fl-muted-note">اطلاعات تکمیلی مانند ترکیبات، مشخصات و روش استفاده هنوز برای این محصول ثبت نشده است.</div>
          )}
        </section>

        {relatedProducts.length > 0 && (
          <section className="container-custom border-t border-[#eee9ef] py-11 md:py-14">
            <div className="fl-section-head">
              <div><span className="fl-section-kicker">پیشنهاد می‌شود</span><h2 className="fl-section-title">محصولات دیگر</h2></div>
              <Link href="/products" className="fl-text-link">مشاهده همه <ArrowUpLeft size={15} /></Link>
            </div>
            <div className="fl-product-grid">
              {relatedProducts.map((item) => (
                <Link key={item.id} href={`/products/${item.slug}`} className="fl-product-card group">
                  <div className="fl-product-media">
                    {getPublicProductImage(item.mainImage) ? (
                      <Image src={getPublicProductImage(item.mainImage)!} alt={`تصویر ${item.name}`} fill unoptimized={Boolean(getPublicProductImage(item.mainImage)?.startsWith('http'))} sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, (max-width: 1024px) 50vw, 25vw" />
                    ) : (
                      <div className="fl-product-placeholder" aria-hidden="true"><span className="absolute text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[.16em] text-[#b77b9a]">فاطمه‌لب</span></div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="fl-product-info">
                    <h3 className="fl-product-name">{item.name}</h3>
                    <div className="fl-product-bottom">
                      <span className="fl-product-price">{item.price > 0 ? formatToman(item.price) : 'به زودی'}</span>
                      <span className={`fl-product-status ${item.stock > 0 && item.price > 0 ? '' : 'is-out'}`}>{item.stock > 0 && item.price > 0 ? 'موجود' : 'به زودی'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <span className="fl-product-view">مشاهده محصول <ArrowLeft size={13} /></span>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}
      </main>
      <Footer />

      {/* استیکی خرید موبایل - اگر قیمت ندارد، مخفی می‌شود */}
      {isPriced && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40 border-t border-[#eee9ef] bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md p-4 md:hidden">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-[#77717b]">قیمت</p>
              <p className="text-[15px] font-extrabold text-[#28242b]">{formatToman(product.price)}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 max-w-[200px]"><AddToCartButton product={product} /></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

==================================================
FILE: src/app/products/[slug]/add-to-cart.tsx
==================================================
'use client';

import { useCartStore } from '@/store/cart';
import { ShoppingBag } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

type CartProduct = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  price: number;
  stock: number;
  mainImage: string | null;
};

export default function AddToCartButton({ product }: { product: CartProduct }) {
  const addItem = useCartStore((state) => state.addItem);
  const hasPrice = product.price > 0;
  const hasStock = product.stock > 0;
  const canPurchase = hasPrice && hasStock;

  const handleAdd = () => {
    if (!hasPrice) { toast.error('این محصول هنوز قیمت‌گذاری نشده است.'); return; }
    if (!hasStock) { toast.error('این محصول در حال حاضر موجود نیست.'); return; }
    addItem({ id: product.id, name: product.name, slug: product.slug, price: product.price, mainImage: product.mainImage });
    toast.success('محصول به سبد خرید اضافه شد.');
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <button 
        type="button" 
        onClick={handleAdd} 
        disabled={!canPurchase} 
        className="btn-primary min-h-[48px] w-full disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:border-[#ddd7de] disabled:bg-[#efedf0] disabled:text-[#8f8791] disabled:shadow-none"
      >
        <ShoppingBag size={17} />
        {canPurchase ? 'افزودن به سبد خرید' : !hasPrice ? 'به زودی عرضه می‌شود' : 'در حال حاضر ناموجود'}
      </button>
      {!canPurchase && (
        <p className="text-center text-[10px] leading-6 text-[#8b818c]">
          {hasPrice ? 'این محصول در حال حاضر قابل سفارش نیست.' : 'امکان سفارش این محصول پس از ثبت قیمت فعال می‌شود.'}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

==================================================
FILE: src/components/storefront/Header.tsx
==================================================
'use client';

import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Menu, Search, ShoppingBag, X, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { useCartStore } from '@/store/cart';

const navigation = [
  { href: '/products', label: 'محصولات' },
  { href: '/about', label: 'درباره فاطمه‌لب' },
  { href: '/journal', label: 'مجله' },
    { href: '/track', label: 'رهگیری سفارش' },
  { href: '/faq', label: 'راهنمای خرید' },
];

export default function Header() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const items = useCartStore((state) => state.items);
  const [isMounted, setIsMounted] = useState(false);
  const [searchOpen, setSearchOpen] = useState(false);
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => setIsMounted(true), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!searchOpen && !mobileMenuOpen) return;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        setSearchOpen(false);
        setMobileMenuOpen(false);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [searchOpen, mobileMenuOpen]);

  const cartCount = isMounted
    ? items.reduce((total, item) => total + item.quantity, 0)
    : 0;

  const closeOverlays = () => {
    setSearchOpen(false);
    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <header className="fl-header">
        <div className="container-custom fl-header-inner">
          <Link href="/" className="fl-brand" aria-label="صفحه اصلی فاطمه‌لب">
            <span className="fl-brand-mark">
              <Image src="/logo.png" alt="نشان فاطمه‌لب" fill sizes="39px" priority />
            </span>
            <span className="flex flex-col">
              <span className="fl-brand-name">فاطمه‌لب</span>
              <span className="fl-brand-latin">FATEMEHLAB</span>
            </span>
          </Link>

          <nav className="fl-nav" aria-label="ناوبری اصلی">
            {navigation.map((item) => (
              <Link
                key={item.href}
                href={item.href}
                aria-current={pathname === item.href ? 'page' : undefined}
              >
                {item.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </nav>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="fl-icon-button"
              aria-label="جست‌وجوی محصولات"
              aria-expanded={searchOpen}
              aria-controls="store-search-panel"
              onClick={() => {
                setSearchOpen((open) => !open);
                setMobileMenuOpen(false);
              }}
            >
              <Search size={18} strokeWidth={1.8} />
            </button>
            <Link
              href="/cart"
              className="fl-icon-button relative"
              aria-label={`سبد خرید${cartCount ? `، ${cartCount} کالا` : ''}`}
            >
              <ShoppingBag size={18} strokeWidth={1.8} />
              {cartCount > 0 && <span className="fl-cart-count">{cartCount.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}</span>}
            </Link>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="fl-icon-button md:hidden"
              aria-label="باز کردن منو"
              aria-expanded={mobileMenuOpen}
              onClick={() => {
                setMobileMenuOpen(true);
                setSearchOpen(false);
              }}
            >
              <Menu size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {searchOpen && (
          <div id="store-search-panel" className="fl-search-panel" role="dialog" aria-label="جست‌وجوی محصولات">
            <form action="/products" method="get" className="fl-search-field" onSubmit={() => setSearchOpen(false)}>
              <Search size={17} className="shrink-0 text-[#a49aa5]" />
              <input
                autoFocus
                name="q"
                type="search"
                placeholder="نام محصول را جست‌وجو کنید..."
                aria-label="عبارت جست‌وجو"
              />
              <button type="submit" className="text-[#c61d70]" aria-label="شروع جست‌وجو">
                <ArrowLeft size={17} />
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}
      </header>

      {mobileMenuOpen && (
        <div className="fl-mobile-nav md:hidden" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="منوی سایت">
          <div className="fl-mobile-nav-head">
            <Link href="/" className="fl-brand" onClick={closeOverlays}>
              <span className="fl-brand-mark">
                <Image src="/logo.png" alt="" fill sizes="39px" />
              </span>
              <span className="flex flex-col">
                <span className="fl-brand-name">فاطمه‌لب</span>
                <span className="fl-brand-latin">FATEMEHLAB</span>
              </span>
            </Link>
            <button type="button" className="fl-icon-button" onClick={closeOverlays} aria-label="بستن منو">
              <X size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>
          <nav className="fl-mobile-nav-links" aria-label="ناوبری موبایل">
            {navigation.map((item) => (
              <Link key={item.href} href={item.href} onClick={closeOverlays}>
                {item.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
            <Link href="/contact" onClick={closeOverlays}>تماس با ما</Link>
            <Link href="/cart" onClick={closeOverlays}>سبد خرید</Link>
          </nav>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

==================================================
FILE: src/components/storefront/Footer.tsx

'use client';

import Image from 'next/image';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { ArrowUpLeft, Mail, Phone, MapPin } from 'lucide-react';
import { InstagramIcon } from '@/components/icons/SocialIcons';

export default function Footer() {
  const [year, setYear] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    setYear(new Date().getFullYear().toLocaleString('fa-IR', { useGrouping: false }));
  }, []);

  return (
    <footer className="fl-footer">
      <div className="container-custom fl-footer-main">
        <div className="fl-footer-brand">
          <Link href="/" className="fl-brand w-fit">
            <span className="fl-brand-mark">
              <Image src="/logo.png" alt="نشان فاطمه‌لب" fill sizes="39px" />
            </span>
            <span className="flex flex-col">
              <span className="fl-brand-name">فاطمه‌لب</span>
              <span className="fl-brand-latin">FATEMEHLAB</span>
            </span>
          </Link>
          <p className="mt-4 max-w-sm text-[12px] leading-[2.1] text-[#77717b]">
            فاطمه‌لب؛ جایی که زیبایی، با آگاهی آغاز می‌شود.
          </p>
          <Link href="/products" className="fl-text-link mt-4">

import { cacheTag } from 'next/cache';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { products } from '@/db/schema';
import { eq, desc } from 'drizzle-orm';

export async function getCachedPublishedProducts() {
  'use cache';
  cacheTag('products');
  return await db
    .select()
    .from(products)
    .where(eq(products.status, 'published'))
    .orderBy(desc(products.createdAt));
}

FILE: src/lib/product-content.ts

const unverifiedSeedDescriptions = new Set([
  'آبرسان و شفاف‌کننده پوست',
  'تقویت‌کننده و ضخیم‌کننده مو',
  'پاک‌سازی عمیق پوست',
  'پوشش طبیعی و ماندگار',
]);

const knownNonProductAssets = [
  'home-services.webp',
  'lumen.png.png',
];

export function getPublicProductDescription(description: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  const value = description?.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  if (!value) return null;
  if (unverifiedSeedDescriptions.has(value)) return null;
  return value;
}

export function getPublicProductImage(image: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!image) return null;
  const normalized = image.toLowerCase().split('?')[0];
  if (knownNonProductAssets.some((asset) => normalized.includes(asset))) return null;
  return image;
}

This is a [Next.js](https://nextjs.org) project bootstrapped with [`create-next-app`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/api-reference/cli/create-next-app).

## Getting Started

First, run the development server:

```bash
npm run dev
# or
yarn dev
# or
pnpm dev
# or
bun dev
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) with your browser to see the result.

You can start editing the page by modifying `app/page.tsx`. The page auto-updates as you edit the file.

This project uses [`next/font`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/optimizing/fonts) to automatically optimize and load [Geist](https://vercel.com/font), a new font family for Vercel.

## Learn More

===== FILE: src\app\account\(protected)\addresses\address-form.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Loader2, Save } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

const PROVINCES = ['تهران', 'اصفهان', 'فارس', 'خراسان رضوی', 'البرز', 'آذربایجان شرقی', 'آذربایجان غربی', 'خوزستان', 'گیلان', 'مازندران', 'کرمان', 'یزد', 'قم', 'قزوین', 'مرکزی', 'همدان', 'کرمانشاه', 'گلستان', 'سیستان و بلوچستان', 'هرمزگان', 'بوشهر', 'اردبیل', 'زنجان', 'سمنان', 'کردستان', 'لرستان', 'چهارمحال و بختیاری', 'کهگیلویه و بویراحمد', 'ایلام', 'خراسان شمالی', 'خراسان جنوبی'];

export default function AddressForm({ initialData, onClose, onSuccess }: { initialData: any, onClose: () => void, onSuccess: () => void }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    title: initialData?.title || '',
    firstName: initialData?.firstName || '',
    lastName: initialData?.lastName || '',
    phone: initialData?.phone || '',
    province: initialData?.province || 'تهران',
    city: initialData?.city || '',
    address: initialData?.address || '',
    postalCode: initialData?.postalCode || '',
    isDefault: initialData?.isDefault || false,
  });

  const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => {
    setFormData({ ...formData, [e.target.name]: e.target.value });
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const url = initialData ? `/api/account/addresses/${initialData.id}` : '/api/account/addresses';
      const method = initialData ? 'PUT' : 'POST';
      const res = await fetch(url, {
        method,
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(formData),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        toast.success(initialData ? 'آدرس ویرایش شد' : 'آدرس افزوده شد');
        onSuccess();
      } else {
        toast.error(data.message || 'خطا');
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error('خطا در ارتباط');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-lg my-8">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-5 border-b border-[#e9e5eb]">
          <h3 className="font-black text-lg">{initialData ? 'ویرایش آدرس' : 'افزودن آدرس جدید'}</h3>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 text-[#77717b] hover:bg-[#f8f7fa] rounded-lg"><X size={20} /></button>
        </div>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-5 space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-1.5 block">عنوان آدرس (مثلاً خانه، محل کار) *</label>
            <input type="text" name="title" value={formData.title} onChange={handleChange} required className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] outline-none text-sm" />
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-1.5 block">نام *</label>
              <input type="text" name="firstName" value={formData.firstName} onChange={handleChange} required className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] outline-none text-sm" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-1.5 block">نام خانوادگی *</label>
              <input type="text" name="lastName" value={formData.lastName} onChange={handleChange} required className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] outline-none text-sm" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-1.5 block">شماره موبایل *</label>
            <input type="tel" name="phone" value={formData.phone} onChange={handleChange} required dir="ltr" className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] outline-none text-sm text-left" />
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-1.5 block">استان *</label>
              <select name="province" value={formData.province} onChange={handleChange} className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] outline-none text-sm cursor-pointer">
                {PROVINCES.map(p => <option key={p} value={p}>{p}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-1.5 block">شهر *</label>
              <input type="text" name="city" value={formData.city} onChange={handleChange} required className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] outline-none text-sm" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-1.5 block">آدرس کامل *</label>
            <textarea name="address" value={formData.address} onChange={handleChange} required rows={3} className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] outline-none text-sm resize-none"></textarea>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-1.5 block">کد پستی *</label>
            <input type="text" name="postalCode" value={formData.postalCode} onChange={handleChange} required dir="ltr" maxLength={10} className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] outline-none text-sm text-left" />
          </div>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={formData.isDefault} onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, isDefault: e.target.checked })} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#c61d70]" />
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#28242b]">این آدرس پیش‌فرض من باشد</span>
          </label>
          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-3">
            <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="flex-1 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#77717b] hover:bg-[#f8f7fa] rounded-xl">انصراف</button>
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-[#c61d70] text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50">
              {loading ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <Save size={16} />} ذخیره
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== END: src\app\account\(protected)\addresses\address-form.tsx =====

===== FILE: src\app\account\(protected)\addresses\address-list.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Plus, MapPin, Edit, Trash2, Star, Loader2, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import AddressForm from './address-form';

export default function AddressList({ initialAddresses }: { initialAddresses: any[] }) {
  const [addresses, setAddresses] = useState(initialAddresses);
  const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingAddress, setEditingAddress] = useState<any>(null);
  const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
    if (!confirm('آیا از حذف این آدرس مطمئن هستید؟')) return;
    setDeletingId(id);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/account/addresses/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      if (res.ok) {
        setAddresses(addresses.filter(a => a.id !== id));
        toast.success('آدرس حذف شد');
        router.refresh();
      } else toast.error('خطا در حذف');
    } catch { toast.error('خطا در ارتباط'); }
    finally { setDeletingId(null); }
  };

  const handleSetDefault = async (id: string) => {
    const addr = addresses.find(a => a.id === id);
    if (!addr) return;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/account/addresses/${id}`, {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ...addr, isDefault: true }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        setAddresses(addresses.map(a => ({ ...a, isDefault: a.id === id })));
        toast.success('آدرس پیش‌فرض تغییر کرد');
        router.refresh();
      } else toast.error('خطا');
    } catch { toast.error('خطا'); }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-[#28242b] flex items-center gap-2">
            <MapPin className="text-[#c61d70]" /> آدرس‌های من
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b] mt-1">{addresses.length.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} آدرس ثبت شده</p>
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => { setEditingAddress(null); setFormOpen(true); }} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-[#c61d70] text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl hover:bg-[#a9155d] transition-colors">
          <Plus size={16} /> آدرس جدید
        </button>
      </div>

      {formOpen && (
        <AddressForm
          initialData={editingAddress}
          onClose={() => { setFormOpen(false); setEditingAddress(null); }}
          onSuccess={() => { setFormOpen(false); setEditingAddress(null); router.refresh(); }}
        />
      )}

      {addresses.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-12 text-center">
          <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-[#fff0f7] rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6">
            <MapPin size={40} className="text-[#c61d70]/40" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-[#28242b] mb-2">هنوز آدرسی ثبت نکرده‌اید</h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b] mb-6">برای ارسال سریع‌تر سفارشات، آدرس خود را ذخیره کنید.</p>
          <button onClick={() => setFormOpen(true)} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-[#c61d70] text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl">
            <Plus size={16} /> افزودن اولین آدرس
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          {addresses.map((address) => (
            <div key={address.id} className={`bg-white rounded-2xl border-2 p-5 transition-all ${address.isDefault ? 'border-[#c61d70] shadow-lg shadow-[#c61d70]/10' : 'border-[#e9e5eb]'}`}>
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="font-bold text-sm text-[#28242b]">{address.title}</span>
                  {address.isDefault && (
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-bold bg-[#c61d70] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Star size={9} fill="currentColor" /> پیش‌فرض
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-1">
                  {!address.isDefault && (
                    <button onClick={() => handleSetDefault(address.id)} className="p-1.5 text-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-50 rounded-lg" title="انتخاب به عنوان پیش‌فرض">
                      <Star size={14} />
                    </button>
                  )}
                  <button onClick={() => { setEditingAddress(address); setFormOpen(true); }} className="p-1.5 text-[#c61d70] hover:bg-[#fff0f7] rounded-lg">
                    <Edit size={14} />
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => handleDelete(address.id)} disabled={deletingId === address.id} className="p-1.5 text-rose-500 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50">
                    {deletingId === address.id ? <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" /> : <Trash2 size={14} />}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-[#77717b] leading-relaxed space-y-1">
                <p className="font-bold text-[#28242b]">{address.firstName} {address.lastName}</p>
                <p dir="ltr" className="font-mono">{address.phone}</p>
                <p>{address.province}، {address.city}</p>
                <p className="line-clamp-2">{address.address}</p>
                <p>کد پستی: <span dir="ltr" className="font-mono">{address.postalCode}</span></p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { addresses } from '@/db/schema';
import { eq, desc } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getCurrentCustomer } from '@/lib/customer-auth';
import AddressList from './address-list';

export const instant = false;

export default async function AddressesPage() {
  const customer = await getCurrentCustomer();
  const list = await db
    .select()
    .from(addresses)
    .where(eq(addresses.customerId, customer!.id))
    .orderBy(desc(addresses.isDefault), desc(addresses.createdAt));

  return <AddressList initialAddresses={list} />;
}

===== FILE: src\app\account\(protected)\layout.tsx =====
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { customers } from '@/db/schema';
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getCurrentCustomer } from '@/lib/customer-auth';
import AccountSidebar from '@/components/account/AccountSidebar';
import AccountBottomNav from '@/components/account/AccountBottomNav';
import AccountMobileHeader from '@/components/account/AccountMobileHeader';

export const instant = false;

export default async function AccountLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const current = await getCurrentCustomer();
  if (!current) redirect('/account/login');

  const [customer] = await db.select().from(customers).where(eq(customers.id, current.id)).limit(1);
  if (!customer) redirect('/account/login');

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#fafafc]">
      {/* سایدبار فقط در دسکتاپ */}
      <AccountSidebar customer={{ name: customer.name, email: customer.email }} />

      {/* محتوای اصلی */}
      <main className="md:mr-64 pb-24 md:pb-8">
        {/* نوار بالای موبایل با دکمه بازگشت به فروشگاه */}
        <AccountMobileHeader />

        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto p-4 md:p-8">
          {children}
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* نوار پایین فقط در موبایل */}
      <AccountBottomNav />
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src\app\account\(protected)\orders\[id]\page.tsx =====
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { orders, orderItems } from '@/db/schema';
import { eq, and } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getCurrentCustomer } from '@/lib/customer-auth';
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { ArrowRight, Package, MapPin, User, Phone, CheckCircle2, Truck, Clock, XCircle, CreditCard } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatToman } from '@/lib/currency';

export const instant = false;

const steps = [
  { id: 'processing', title: 'در حال پردازش', icon: Package },
  { id: 'shipped', title: 'ارسال شده', icon: Truck },
  { id: 'delivered', title: 'تحویل شده', icon: CheckCircle2 },
];

export default async function OrderDetailPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const customer = await getCurrentCustomer();
  
  const [order] = await db
    .select()
    .from(orders)
    .where(and(eq(orders.id, id), eq(orders.customerId, customer!.id)))
    .limit(1);

  if (!order) notFound();

  const items = await db.select().from(orderItems).where(eq(orderItems.orderId, id));

  const getStepIndex = (status: string) => {
    if (status === 'cancelled') return -1;
    return steps.findIndex((s) => s.id === status);
  };

  const currentStep = getStepIndex(order.status);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <Link href="/account/orders" className="p-2 bg-white border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl hover:bg-[#f8f7fa] transition-colors">
          <ArrowRight size={18} />
        </Link>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-[#28242b]">جزئیات سفارش</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b] font-mono" dir="ltr">{order.trackingCode}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* وضعیت و تایم‌لاین */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-6 md:p-8 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8 pb-6 border-b border-[#e9e5eb]">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#77717b] mb-1">تاریخ ثبت</p>
            <p className="font-bold text-sm">{new Date(order.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('fa-IR')}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="text-left">
            <p className="text-xs text-[#77717b] mb-1">مبلغ کل</p>
            <p className="font-black text-lg text-[#c61d70]">{formatToman(order.totalAmount)}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {order.status === 'cancelled' ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-rose-700">
            <XCircle size={20} />
            <span className="font-bold text-sm">این سفارش لغو شده است</span>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="relative">
            <div className="absolute top-5 right-0 left-0 h-1 bg-[#f5f0f2] rounded-full -z-10"></div>
            <div
              className="absolute top-5 right-0 h-1 bg-[#c61d70] rounded-full transition-all duration-1000 -z-10"
              style={{ width: `${(currentStep / (steps.length - 1)) * 100}%` }}
            ></div>
            <div className="flex justify-between">
              {steps.map((step, index) => {
                const isCompleted = currentStep >= index;
                const isActive = currentStep === index;
                return (
                  <div key={step.id} className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 w-1/3">
                    <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center border-2 bg-white transition-all ${isCompleted ? 'border-[#c61d70] text-[#c61d70] shadow-lg shadow-[#c61d70]/20' : 'border-[#e9e5eb] text-[#918a95]'}`}>
                      <step.icon size={18} />
                    </div>
                    <span className={`text-[10px] md:text-xs font-bold text-center ${isActive ? 'text-[#c61d70]' : isCompleted ? 'text-[#28242b]' : 'text-[#918a95]'}`}>
                      {step.title}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* محصولات */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-6 md:p-8 shadow-sm">
        <h2 className="font-black text-lg text-[#28242b] mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><Package size={20} className="text-[#c61d70]" /> محصولات سفارش</h2>
        <div className="divide-y divide-[#e9e5eb]">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div key={item.id} className="flex items-center justify-between py-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-black">{item.quantity.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}×</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-sm text-[#28242b]">{item.productName}</span>
              </div>
              <span className="font-bold text-sm text-[#28242b]">{formatToman(item.price * item.quantity)}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* اطلاعات گیرنده */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-6 md:p-8 shadow-sm">
        <h2 className="font-black text-lg text-[#28242b] mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><MapPin size={20} className="text-[#c61d70]" /> اطلاعات ارسال</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><User size={16} className="text-[#918a95]" /> {order.firstName} {order.lastName}</div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Phone size={16} className="text-[#918a95]" /> <span dir="ltr">{order.phone}</span></div>
          <div className="md:col-span-2 flex items-start gap-2 text-[#77717b] leading-relaxed">
            <MapPin size={16} className="text-[#918a95] mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
            <span>{order.city}، {order.address} (کد پستی: {order.postalCode})</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
