===== FILE: src\app\account\(protected)\orders\page.tsx =====
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { orders } from '@/db/schema';
import { eq, desc } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getCurrentCustomer } from '@/lib/customer-auth';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Package, ArrowLeft, Clock, CheckCircle2, Truck, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatToman } from '@/lib/currency';

export const instant = false;

const statusMap: Record<string, { label: string; color: string; icon: any }> = {
  pending: { label: 'در انتظار پرداخت', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700', icon: Clock },
  processing: { label: 'در حال پردازش', color: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700', icon: Package },
  shipped: { label: 'ارسال شده', color: 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700', icon: Truck },
  delivered: { label: 'تحویل شده', color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700', icon: CheckCircle2 },
  cancelled: { label: 'لغو شده', color: 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700', icon: XCircle },
};

export default async function CustomerOrdersPage() {
  const customer = await getCurrentCustomer();
  const myOrders = await db
    .select()
    .from(orders)
    .where(eq(orders.customerId, customer!.id))
    .orderBy(desc(orders.createdAt));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-[#28242b] flex items-center gap-2">
          <Package className="text-[#c61d70]" /> سفارشات من
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b] mt-1">{myOrders.length.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} سفارش ثبت شده</p>
      </div>

      {myOrders.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-12 text-center">
          <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-[#fff0f7] rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6">
            <Package size={40} className="text-[#c61d70]/40" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-[#28242b] mb-2">هنوز سفارشی ثبت نکرده‌اید</h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b] mb-6">با مرور محصولات، اولین سفارش خود را ثبت کنید.</p>
          <Link href="/products" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-[#c61d70] text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl hover:bg-[#a9155d] transition-colors">
            شروع خرید <ArrowLeft size={16} />
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          {myOrders.map((order) => {
            const status = statusMap[order.status] || { label: order.status, color: 'bg-gray-50 text-gray-700', icon: Package };
            const StatusIcon = status.icon;
            return (
              <Link
                key={order.id}
                href={`/account/orders/${order.id}`}
                className="group bg-white rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-5 hover:shadow-lg hover:border-[#c61d70]/30 transition-all"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                      <span className="font-mono text-sm md:text-base font-black text-[#28242b]" dir="ltr">
                        {order.trackingCode}
                      </span>
                      <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full font-bold ${status.color}`}>
                        <StatusIcon size={11} /> {status.label}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-1 text-xs text-[#77717b]">
                      <span>تاریخ: {new Date(order.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('fa-IR')}</span>
                      <span>مبلغ: {formatToman(order.totalAmount)}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[#c61d70] font-bold text-sm group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform">
                    مشاهده جزئیات <ArrowLeft size={16} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Link>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
===== END: src\app\account\(protected)\orders\page.tsx =====

===== FILE: src\app\account\(protected)\page.tsx =====
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { orders, wishlist, addresses } from '@/db/schema';
import { eq, desc } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getCurrentCustomer } from '@/lib/customer-auth';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Package, Heart, MapPin, ArrowLeft, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatToman } from '@/lib/currency';

export const instant = false;

const statusLabels: Record<string, { label: string; color: string }> = {
  pending: { label: 'در انتظار', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700' },
  processing: { label: 'در حال پردازش', color: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700' },
  shipped: { label: 'ارسال شده', color: 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700' },
  delivered: { label: 'تحویل شده', color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700' },
  cancelled: { label: 'لغو شده', color: 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700' },
};

export default async function AccountDashboard() {
  const current = await getCurrentCustomer();
  const customerId = current!.id;

  const myOrders = await db.select().from(orders).where(eq(orders.customerId, customerId)).orderBy(desc(orders.createdAt)).limit(3);
  const myWishlist = await db.select({ id: wishlist.id }).from(wishlist).where(eq(wishlist.customerId, customerId));
  const myAddresses = await db.select({ id: addresses.id }).from(addresses).where(eq(addresses.customerId, customerId));

  const stats = [
    { title: 'سفارشات', value: myOrders.length, icon: Package, href: '/account/orders', color: 'from-blue-500 to-blue-600' },
    { title: 'علاقه‌مندی‌ها', value: myWishlist.length, icon: Heart, href: '/account/wishlist', color: 'from-rose-500 to-pink-600' },
    { title: 'آدرس‌ها', value: myAddresses.length, icon: MapPin, href: '/account/addresses', color: 'from-amber-500 to-orange-600' },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-[#28242b]">سلام {current!.name.split(' ')[0]}! 👋</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b] mt-1">به داشبورد خود خوش آمدید. از اینجا می‌توانید سفارشات، علاقه‌مندی‌ها و اطلاعات خود را مدیریت کنید.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {stats.map((s) => (
          <Link key={s.title} href={s.href} className="group bg-white border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-2xl p-5 hover:shadow-lg hover:shadow-[#c61d70]/5 transition-all">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
              <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br ${s.color} text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg`}>
                <s.icon size={22} />
              </div>
              <ArrowLeft size={16} className="text-[#c61d70] opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity" />
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b] mb-1">{s.title}</p>
            <p className="text-3xl font-black text-[#28242b]">{s.value.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}</p>
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-2xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-[#e9e5eb] flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="font-black text-lg text-[#28242b] flex items-center gap-2">
            <Clock size={20} className="text-[#c61d70]" /> آخرین سفارشات
          </h2>
          <Link href="/account/orders" className="text-xs font-bold text-[#c61d70] hover:underline">
            مشاهده همه
          </Link>
        </div>

        {myOrders.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-10 text-center">
            <Package size={48} className="mx-auto text-[#c61d70]/30 mb-3" />
            <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b] mb-4">هنوز سفارشی ثبت نکرده‌اید</p>
            <Link href="/products" className="inline-block px-6 py-2.5 bg-[#c61d70] text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl">
              شروع خرید
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="divide-y divide-[#e9e5eb]">
            {myOrders.map((order) => {
              const status = statusLabels[order.status] || { label: order.status, color: 'bg-gray-50 text-gray-700' };
              return (
                <Link key={order.id} href={`/account/orders/${order.id}`} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 hover:bg-[#f8f7fa] transition-colors">
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-mono text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b]" dir="ltr">{order.trackingCode}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-[#918a95] mt-1">{new Date(order.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('fa-IR')}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-left">
                    <p className="font-black text-[#28242b] text-sm">{formatToman(order.totalAmount)}</p>
                    <span className={`text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold ${status.color}`}>{status.label}</span>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== END: src\app\account\(protected)\page.tsx =====

===== FILE: src\app\account\(protected)\profile\page.tsx =====
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { customers, orders, wishlist, addresses } from '@/db/schema';
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getCurrentCustomer } from '@/lib/customer-auth';
import ProfileClient from './profile-client';

export const instant = false;

export default async function ProfilePage() {
  const current = await getCurrentCustomer();
  const [customer] = await db.select().from(customers).where(eq(customers.id, current!.id)).limit(1);

  const myOrders = await db.select({ id: orders.id }).from(orders).where(eq(orders.customerId, current!.id));
  const myWishlist = await db.select({ id: wishlist.id }).from(wishlist).where(eq(wishlist.customerId, current!.id));
  const myAddresses = await db.select({ id: addresses.id }).from(addresses).where(eq(addresses.customerId, current!.id));

  const stats = {
    orders: myOrders.length,
    wishlist: myWishlist.length,
    addresses: myAddresses.length,
    memberSince: customer.createdAt,
  };

  return <ProfileClient customer={customer} stats={stats} />;
}
===== END: src\app\account\(protected)\profile\page.tsx =====

===== FILE: src\app\account\(protected)\profile\profile-client.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import {
  Camera, User, Mail, Phone, Loader2, Save, ShieldCheck,
  Package, Heart, MapPin, Calendar, Lock, Eye, EyeOff, Check,
  ArrowLeft, Sparkles, Award, Clock, LogOut
} from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

interface ProfileClientProps {
  customer: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    phone: string | null;
    avatar: string | null;
    createdAt: Date;
  };
  stats: {
    orders: number;
    wishlist: number;
    addresses: number;
    memberSince: Date;
  };
}

export default function ProfileClient({ customer, stats }: ProfileClientProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState(customer.avatar);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ name: customer.name, phone: customer.phone || '' });
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [passwordData, setPasswordData] = useState({ current: '', newPass: '', confirm: '' });
  const [changingPassword, setChangingPassword] = useState(false);
  const [showPasswords, setShowPasswords] = useState({ current: false, newPass: false, confirm: false });

  const handleAvatarChange = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    if (file.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
      toast.error('حجم عکس حداکثر ۲ مگابایت است');
      return;
    }
    setUploading(true);
    const fd = new FormData();
    fd.append('file', file);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/account/avatar', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        setAvatar(data.avatar);
        toast.success('عکس پروفایل به‌روزرسانی شد');
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        toast.error(data.message || 'خطا در آپلود');
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error('خطا در ارتباط با سرور');
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleSaveProfile = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/account/profile', {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(formData),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        toast.success('اطلاعات شما با موفقیت ذخیره شد');
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        toast.error(data.message || 'خطا');
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error('خطا در ارتباط با سرور');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleChangePassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (passwordData.newPass !== passwordData.confirm) {
      toast.error('رمز جدید با تکرار آن یکسان نیست');
      return;
    }
    if (passwordData.newPass.length < 6) {
      toast.error('رمز جدید باید حداقل ۶ حرف باشد');
      return;
    }
    setChangingPassword(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/account/change-password', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          currentPassword: passwordData.current,
          newPassword: passwordData.newPass,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        toast.success('رمز عبور با موفقیت تغییر کرد');
        setPasswordData({ current: '', newPass: '', confirm: '' });
      } else {
        toast.error(data.message || 'خطا');
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error('خطا در ارتباط با سرور');
    } finally {
      setChangingPassword(false);
    }
  };

  const memberDays = Math.floor(
    (Date.now() - new Date(stats.memberSince).getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)
  );

  const getMembershipLevel = () => {
    if (memberDays >= 365) return { title: 'کاربر طلایی', color: 'text-amber-600', bg: 'bg-amber-50', border: 'border-amber-200' };
    if (memberDays >= 90) return { title: 'کاربر نقره‌ای', color: 'text-slate-600', bg: 'bg-slate-50', border: 'border-slate-200' };
    if (memberDays >= 30) return { title: 'کاربر برنزی', color: 'text-orange-700', bg: 'bg-orange-50', border: 'border-orange-200' };
    return { title: 'کاربر جدید', color: 'text-[#c61d70]', bg: 'bg-[#fff0f7]', border: 'border-[#f2d7e5]' };
  };

  const level = getMembershipLevel();

  const statCards = [
    { label: 'سفارشات', value: stats.orders, icon: Package, gradient: 'from-blue-500 to-blue-600', href: '/account/orders' },
    { label: 'علاقه‌مندی', value: stats.wishlist, icon: Heart, gradient: 'from-rose-500 to-pink-600', href: '/account/wishlist' },
    { label: 'آدرس‌ها', value: stats.addresses, icon: MapPin, gradient: 'from-amber-500 to-orange-600', href: '/account/addresses' },
    { label: 'روز عضویت', value: memberDays, icon: Calendar, gradient: 'from-emerald-500 to-teal-600', href: null },
  ];

  const passwordFields = [
    { key: 'current', label: 'رمز عبور فعلی', value: passwordData.current, onChange: (v: string) => setPasswordData({ ...passwordData, current: v }) },
    { key: 'newPass', label: 'رمز عبور جدید', value: passwordData.newPass, onChange: (v: string) => setPasswordData({ ...passwordData, newPass: v }) },
    { key: 'confirm', label: 'تکرار رمز جدید', value: passwordData.confirm, onChange: (v: string) => setPasswordData({ ...passwordData, confirm: v }) },
  ] as const;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5 md:space-y-6">

      {/* ===== Hero ===== */}
      <div className="relative bg-gradient-to-br from-[#fff0f7] via-[#fce5f0] to-[#f8f7fa] rounded-3xl p-5 md:p-10 border border-[#f2d7e5] overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-64 h-64 bg-[#c61d70]/10 rounded-full blur-3xl -translate-y-1/2 translate-x-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-48 h-48 bg-[#c61d70]/10 rounded-full blur-3xl translate-y-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 pointer-events-none" />

        <div className="relative flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center md:items-end gap-4 md:gap-6">
          {/* Avatar */}
          <div className="relative group">
            <div className="w-28 h-28 md:w-36 md:h-36 rounded-full overflow-hidden border-4 border-white shadow-xl bg-white">
              {avatar ? (
                <Image
                  src={avatar}
                  alt={customer.name}
                  width={144}
                  height={144}
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="w-full h-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#c61d70] to-[#e84e9e] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-5xl font-black">
                  {customer.name.charAt(0)}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              disabled={uploading}
              className="absolute bottom-1 right-1 w-10 h-10 bg-[#c61d70] text-white rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg hover:scale-110 active:scale-95 transition-transform border-2 border-white disabled:opacity-50"
              aria-label="تغییر عکس پروفایل"
            >
              {uploading ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <Camera size={16} />}
            </button>
            <input
              ref={fileInputRef}
              type="file"
              accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp"
              onChange={handleAvatarChange}
              disabled={uploading}
              className="hidden"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Info */}
          <div className="flex-1 text-center md:text-right pb-2">
            <h1 className="text-xl md:text-3xl font-black text-[#28242b] mb-1 md:mb-2">{customer.name}</h1>
            <p className="text-xs md:text-sm text-[#77717b] mb-3" dir="ltr">{customer.email}</p>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center md:justify-start gap-2">
              <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] md:text-xs ${level.color} ${level.bg} px-3 py-1 rounded-full font-bold border ${level.border}`}>
                <Award size={12} /> {level.title}
              </span>
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] md:text-xs text-emerald-600 bg-white px-3 py-1 rounded-full font-bold border border-emerald-200">
                <ShieldCheck size={12} /> حساب فعال
              </span>
              {customer.phone && (
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] md:text-xs text-[#77717b] bg-white px-3 py-1 rounded-full font-bold border border-[#e9e5eb]" dir="ltr">
                  <Phone size={12} /> {customer.phone}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Desktop logout button */}
          <Link
            href="/"
            className="hidden md:inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-white/80 border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl text-[#28242b] text-xs font-bold hover:bg-white transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={14} /> بازگشت به فروشگاه
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ===== Stats ===== */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-2 md:gap-3">
        {statCards.map((s) => {
          const Icon = s.icon;
          const content = (
            <>
              <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br ${s.gradient} text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-md mb-3`}>
                <Icon size={18} />
              </div>
              <p className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-[#28242b]">{s.value.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] md:text-xs text-[#77717b] font-medium mt-1">{s.label}</p>
            </>
          );

          if (s.href) {
            return (
              <Link
                key={s.label}
                href={s.href}
                className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-3 md:p-4 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:border-[#c61d70]/40 transition-all"
              >
                {content}
              </Link>
            );
          }
          return (
            <div key={s.label} className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-3 md:p-4 shadow-sm">
              {content}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* ===== Two column forms ===== */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-5 md:gap-6">

        {/* Personal Info */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-5 md:p-8 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-5 md:mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e9e5eb]">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-[#fff0f7] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <User size={20} className="text-[#c61d70]" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-black text-base md:text-lg text-[#28242b]">اطلاعات شخصی</h2>
              <p className="text-[11px] md:text-xs text-[#77717b]">نام و شماره تماس خود را ویرایش کنید</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleSaveProfile} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2 block">نام و نام خانوادگی</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <User className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={formData.name}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, name: e.target.value })}
                  required
                  className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3 md:py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#c61d70]/20 outline-none text-sm md:text-base transition-all"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2 block">ایمیل (غیرقابل تغییر)</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
                <input
                  type="email"
                  value={customer.email}
                  disabled
                  dir="ltr"
                  className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3 md:py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl text-sm md:text-base text-left text-[#918a95] cursor-not-allowed"
                />
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-[#918a95] mt-1.5">برای تغییر ایمیل با پشتیبانی تماس بگیرید</p>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2 block">شماره موبایل</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Phone className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
                <input
                  type="tel"
                  value={formData.phone}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, phone: e.target.value })}
                  dir="ltr"
                  placeholder="09xxxxxxxxx"
                  className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3 md:py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#c61d70]/20 outline-none text-sm md:text-base transition-all text-left"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-[#c61d70] text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-[#c61d70]/30 hover:bg-[#a9155d] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {saving ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <Save size={16} />}
              {saving ? 'در حال ذخیره...' : 'ذخیره تغییرات'}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* Change Password */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-5 md:p-8 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-5 md:mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e9e5eb]">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-[#fff0f7] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
              <Lock size={20} className="text-[#c61d70]" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-black text-base md:text-lg text-[#28242b]">تغییر رمز عبور</h2>
              <p className="text-[11px] md:text-xs text-[#77717b]">رمز جدید باید حداقل ۶ حرف باشد</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleChangePassword} className="space-y-4">
            {passwordFields.map((field) => {
              const isConfirmValid =
                field.key === 'confirm' &&
                passwordData.confirm &&
                passwordData.confirm === passwordData.newPass;

              return (
                <div key={field.key}>
                  <label className="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2 block">{field.label}</label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Lock className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
                    <input
                      type={showPasswords[field.key] ? 'text' : 'password'}
                      value={field.value}
                      onChange={(e) => field.onChange(e.target.value)}
                      required
                      minLength={field.key === 'newPass' ? 6 : undefined}
                      className={`w-full pr-12 pl-12 py-3 md:py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:ring-2 outline-none text-sm md:text-base transition-all ${
                        isConfirmValid
                          ? 'border-emerald-400 focus:border-emerald-500 focus:ring-emerald-500/20'
                          : 'border-[#e9e5eb] focus:border-[#c61d70] focus:ring-[#c61d70]/20'
                      }`}
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setShowPasswords({ ...showPasswords, [field.key]: !showPasswords[field.key] })}
                      className="absolute left-4 top-3.5 text-[#918a95] hover:text-[#c61d70] transition-colors"
                      aria-label={showPasswords[field.key] ? 'مخفی کردن رمز' : 'نمایش رمز'}
                    >
                      {showPasswords[field.key] ? <EyeOff size={18} /> : <Eye size={18} />}
                    </button>
                    {isConfirmValid && (
                      <Check size={18} className="absolute left-12 top-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={changingPassword}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-[#28242b] text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl hover:bg-[#3a333f] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {changingPassword ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <Lock size={16} />}
              {changingPassword ? 'در حال تغییر...' : 'تغییر رمز عبور'}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ===== Membership Info ===== */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-[#28242b] to-[#3a333f] rounded-3xl p-5 md:p-8 text-white overflow-hidden relative">
        <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-40 h-40 bg-[#c61d70]/30 rounded-full blur-3xl -translate-y-1/2 translate-x-1/2" />
        <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-40 h-40 bg-white/5 rounded-full blur-3xl translate-y-1/2 -translate-x-1/2" />

        <div className="relative">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
            <Sparkles size={20} className="text-[#f4b7d5]" />
            <h3 className="font-black text-base md:text-lg">باشگاه مشتریان فاطمه بیوتی لند</h3>
          </div>

          <p className="text-xs md:text-sm text-white/70 leading-relaxed mb-5">
            به عنوان عضو فعال، شما به تخفیف‌های ویژه، پیشنهادهای اختصاصی و اطلاع‌رسانی زودتر محصولات جدید دسترسی دارید.
          </p>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            <div className="bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-xl p-3 border border-white/10">
              <Clock size={16} className="text-[#f4b7d5] mb-2" />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-white/60 mb-1">عضویت شما</p>
              <p className="text-sm font-black">{memberDays.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} روز</p>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-xl p-3 border border-white/10">
              <Award size={16} className="text-[#f4b7d5] mb-2" />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-white/60 mb-1">سطح فعلی</p>
              <p className="text-sm font-black">{level.title}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-xl p-3 border border-white/10">
              <Calendar size={16} className="text-[#f4b7d5] mb-2" />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-white/60 mb-1">تاریخ عضویت</p>
              <p className="text-sm font-black">
                {new Date(stats.memberSince).toLocaleDateString('fa-IR')}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ===== Mobile Logout (only visible on mobile) ===== */}
      <div className="md:hidden">
        <Link
          href="/"
          className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 w-full py-3 bg-white border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl text-[#28242b] text-sm font-bold active:scale-[0.98] transition-transform"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={16} /> بازگشت به فروشگاه
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src\app\account\(protected)\profile\profile-form.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { User, Mail, Phone, Loader2, Save, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export default function ProfileForm({ customer }: { customer: any }) {
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ name: customer.name, phone: customer.phone || '' });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/account/profile', {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(formData),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        toast.success('اطلاعات با موفقیت ذخیره شد');
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        toast.error(data.message || 'خطا');
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error('خطا در ارتباط');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-[#28242b] flex items-center gap-2">
          <User className="text-[#c61d70]" /> پروفایل من
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b] mt-1">اطلاعات شخصی خود را ویرایش کنید</p>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-6 md:p-8 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8 pb-6 border-b border-[#e9e5eb]">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 md:w-20 md:h-20 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#c61d70] to-[#e84e9e] text-white flex items-center justify-center text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black shadow-lg">
            {customer.name.charAt(0)}
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-lg md:text-xl font-black text-[#28242b]">{customer.name}</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b]" dir="ltr">{customer.email}</p>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold mt-2">
              <ShieldCheck size={10} /> حساب تایید شده
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2 block">نام و نام خانوادگی</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <User className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={formData.name}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, name: e.target.value })}
                required
                className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#c61d70]/20 outline-none text-base transition-all"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2 block">ایمیل (غیرقابل تغییر)</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Mail className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
              <input
                type="email"
                value={customer.email}
                disabled
                dir="ltr"
                className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl text-base text-left text-[#918a95] cursor-not-allowed"
              />
            </div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-[#918a95] mt-1">برای تغییر ایمیل با پشتیبانی تماس بگیرید</p>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2 block">شماره موبایل</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Phone className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
              <input
                type="tel"
                value={formData.phone}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, phone: e.target.value })}
                dir="ltr"
                placeholder="09xxxxxxxxx"
                className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#c61d70]/20 outline-none text-base transition-all text-left"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-8 py-3 bg-[#c61d70] text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-[#c61d70]/30 hover:bg-[#a9155d] transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <Save size={16} />}
            {loading ? 'در حال ذخیره...' : 'ذخیره تغییرات'}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src\app\account\(protected)\wishlist\page.tsx =====
import { db } from '@/lib/db';
import { wishlist, products } from '@/db/schema';
import { eq, desc, inArray } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { getCurrentCustomer } from '@/lib/customer-auth';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { Heart, Package, ArrowLeft, ShoppingBag } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatToman } from '@/lib/currency';
import { getPublicProductImage } from '@/lib/product-content';
import WishlistRemoveButton from './remove-button';

export const instant = false;

export default async function WishlistPage() {
  const customer = await getCurrentCustomer();
  const customerId = customer!.id;

  const items = await db
    .select({
      id: wishlist.id,
      productId: wishlist.productId,
      createdAt: wishlist.createdAt,
    })
    .from(wishlist)
    .where(eq(wishlist.customerId, customerId))
    .orderBy(desc(wishlist.createdAt));

  let productsList: any[] = [];
  if (items.length > 0) {
    const productIds = items.map(i => i.productId);
    productsList = await db.select().from(products).where(inArray(products.id, productIds));
  }

  const mergedItems = items
    .map(item => ({
      ...item,
      product: productsList.find(p => p.id === item.productId),
    }))
    .filter(item => item.product);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-[#28242b] flex items-center gap-2">
            <Heart className="text-rose-500" fill="currentColor" />
            علاقه‌مندی‌های من
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b] mt-1">
            {mergedItems.length.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} محصول در لیست علاقه‌مندی‌های شما
          </p>
        </div>
        <Link href="/products" className="hidden md:inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-[#c61d70] text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl hover:bg-[#a9155d] transition-colors">
          <ShoppingBag size={16} /> ادامه خرید
        </Link>
      </div>

      {mergedItems.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-[#e9e5eb] p-12 text-center">
          <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-rose-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6">
            <Heart size={40} className="text-rose-300" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-[#28242b] mb-2">لیست علاقه‌مندی‌ها خالی است</h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b] mb-6 max-w-md mx-auto">
            محصولاتی که به آن‌ها علاقه دارید را با کلیک روی آیکون قلب ذخیره کنید و بعداً به راحتی پیدایشان کنید.
          </p>
          <Link href="/products" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-[#c61d70] text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl hover:bg-[#a9155d] transition-colors">
            مشاهده محصولات <ArrowLeft size={16} />
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3 md:gap-5">
          {mergedItems.map((item) => {
            const product = item.product!;
            const hasPrice = product.price > 0;
            const isAvailable = product.stock > 0 && hasPrice;
            const productImage = getPublicProductImage(product.mainImage);

            return (
              <div key={item.id} className="group bg-white rounded-2xl border border-[#e9e5eb] overflow-hidden hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-[#c61d70]/5 transition-all">
                <Link href={`/products/${product.slug}`} className="block">
                  <div className="relative aspect-square bg-[#fafafc] p-4">
                    {productImage ? (
                      <Image src={productImage} alt={product.name} fill unoptimized={productImage.startsWith('http')} className="object-contain transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:scale-105" />
                    ) : (
                      <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-[#c61d70]/20"><Package size={32} /></div>
                    )}
                    {!isAvailable && (
                      <span className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-[9px] font-bold text-rose-500 shadow-sm">
                        {hasPrice ? 'ناموجود' : 'در انتظار قیمت'}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="p-4">
                    <h3 className="text-sm md:text-base font-black text-[#28242b] mb-1 line-clamp-1 group-hover:text-[#c61d70] transition-colors">{product.name}</h3>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-[#f5f0f2] pt-3 mt-2">
                      <span className="text-xs md:text-sm font-black text-[#28242b]">{hasPrice ? formatToman(product.price) : '---'}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </Link>
                <div className="px-4 pb-4 flex gap-2">
                  <Link href={`/products/${product.slug}`} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 bg-[#c61d70] text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-[#a9155d] transition-colors">
                    <ShoppingBag size={13} /> مشاهده
                  </Link>
                  <WishlistRemoveButton wishlistId={item.id} />
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      <Link href="/products" className="md:hidden flex items-center justify-center gap-2 w-full py-3 bg-[#c61d70] text-white text-sm font-bold rounded-xl hover:bg-[#a9155d] transition-colors">
        <ShoppingBag size={16} /> ادامه خرید
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}
===== END: src\app\account\(protected)\wishlist\page.tsx =====

===== FILE: src\app\account\(protected)\wishlist\remove-button.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Trash2, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export default function WishlistRemoveButton({ wishlistId }: { wishlistId: string }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleRemove = async () => {
    if (loading) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/account/wishlist?id=${wishlistId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      if (res.ok) {
        toast.success('از علاقه‌مندی‌ها حذف شد');
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        toast.error('خطا در حذف');
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error('خطا در ارتباط با سرور');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={handleRemove}
      disabled={loading}
      className="flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 bg-rose-50 text-rose-500 rounded-lg hover:bg-rose-100 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
      aria-label="حذف از علاقه‌مندی‌ها"
    >
      {loading ? <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" /> : <Trash2 size={14} />}
    </button>
  );
}
===== END: src\app\account\(protected)\wishlist\remove-button.tsx =====

===== FILE: src\app\account\(public)\login\page.tsx =====
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Mail, Lock, Loader2, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export default function CustomerLoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/account/login', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        toast.success('خوش آمدید!');
        router.push('/account');
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        toast.error(data.message || 'خطا در ورود');
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error('خطا در ارتباط با سرور');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-[#fafafc] via-white to-[#fff0f7] px-4 py-12">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md">
        <Link href="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#c61d70] mb-6 hover:underline">
          <ArrowLeft size={14} /> بازگشت به فروشگاه
        </Link>
        
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl shadow-[#c61d70]/10 border border-[#e9e5eb] p-8 md:p-10">
          <div className="text-center mb-8">
            <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-[#28242b] mb-2">ورود به حساب کاربری</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b]">برای مشاهده سفارشات و علاقه‌مندی‌ها وارد شوید</p>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2">ایمیل</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
                <input
                  type="email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  dir="ltr"
                  placeholder="you@example.com"
                  className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#c61d70]/20 outline-none text-base transition-all text-left"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2">رمز عبور</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Lock className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
                <input
                  type="password"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#c61d70]/20 outline-none text-base transition-all"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-3.5 bg-[#c61d70] text-white font-bold rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-[#c61d70]/30 hover:bg-[#a9155d] transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loading ? <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
              {loading ? 'در حال ورود...' : 'ورود به حساب'}
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="mt-6 pt-6 border-t border-[#e9e5eb] text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b]">

                ثبت‌نام کنید
              </Link>
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src\app\account\(public)\register\page.tsx =====
'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Mail, Lock, Loader2, ArrowLeft, User } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export default function CustomerRegisterPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleRegister = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      toast.error('رمز عبور و تکرار آن یکسان نیستند');
      return;
    }
    if (password.length < 6) {
      toast.error('رمز عبور باید حداقل ۶ حرف باشد');
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/account/register', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ name, email, password }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        toast.success('ثبت‌نام با موفقیت انجام شد');
        router.push('/account');
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        toast.error(data.message || 'خطا در ثبت‌نام');
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error('خطا در ارتباط با سرور');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-[#fafafc] via-white to-[#fff0f7] px-4 py-12">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md">
        <Link href="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#c61d70] mb-6 hover:underline">
          <ArrowLeft size={14} /> بازگشت به فروشگاه
        </Link>
        
        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl shadow-[#c61d70]/10 border border-[#e9e5eb] p-8 md:p-10">
          <div className="text-center mb-8">
            <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black text-[#28242b] mb-2">ساخت حساب کاربری</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b]">در چند ثانیه ثبت‌نام کنید</p>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleRegister} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2">نام و نام خانوادگی</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <User className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={name}
                  onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  placeholder="مثال: فاطمه محمدی"
                  className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#c61d70]/20 outline-none text-base transition-all"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2">ایمیل</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
                <input
                  type="email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  dir="ltr"
                  placeholder="you@example.com"
                  className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#c61d70]/20 outline-none text-base transition-all text-left"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2">رمز عبور</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Lock className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
                <input
                  type="password"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  minLength={6}
                  placeholder="حداقل ۶ حرف"
                  className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#c61d70]/20 outline-none text-base transition-all"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-[#28242b] mb-2">تکرار رمز عبور</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Lock className="absolute right-4 top-3.5 w-5 h-5 text-[#918a95]" />
                <input
                  type="password"
                  value={confirmPassword}
                  onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full pr-12 pl-4 py-3.5 bg-[#f8f7fa] border border-[#e9e5eb] rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:border-[#c61d70] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#c61d70]/20 outline-none text-base transition-all"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-3.5 bg-[#c61d70] text-white font-bold rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-[#c61d70]/30 hover:bg-[#a9155d] transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loading ? <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
              {loading ? 'در حال ثبت‌نام...' : 'ساخت حساب'}
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="mt-6 pt-6 border-t border-[#e9e5eb] text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-[#77717b]">
              قبلاً ثبت‌نام کرده‌اید؟{' '}
              <Link href="/account/login" className="font-bold text-[#c61d70] hover:underline">
                وارد شوید
              </Link>
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src\app\admin\(dashboard)\admin-layout-client.tsx =====
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { usePathname, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { LayoutDashboard, ShoppingBag, LogOut, Settings, Menu, X, Package, BookOpen, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Toaster } from 'sonner';
import AdminBottomNav from '@/components/admin/AdminBottomNav';

export default function AdminLayoutClient({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [isMobileMenuOpen, setIsMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [pendingCount, setPendingCount] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => { setIsMobileMenuOpen(false); }, [pathname]);
  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.style.overflow = isMobileMenuOpen ? 'hidden' : 'unset';
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = 'unset'; };
  }, [isMobileMenuOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const readOrders = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('readOrders') || '[]');
    fetch('/api/admin/orders').then(res => res.json()).then(data => {
      if (data.success) {
        setPendingCount(data.orders.filter((o: any) => o.status === 'processing' && !readOrders.includes(o.id)).length);
      }
    }).catch(() => {});
  }, [pathname]);

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    try { await fetch('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' }); } catch (e) {}
    router.push('/admin/login'); router.refresh();
  };

  const menuItems = [
    { name: 'داشبورد', href: '/admin', icon: LayoutDashboard },
    { name: 'محصولات', href: '/admin/products', icon: ShoppingBag },
    { name: 'مجله', href: '/admin/articles', icon: BookOpen },
    { name: 'نظرات', href: '/admin/reviews', icon: MessageSquare },
    { name: 'سفارشات', href: '/admin/orders', icon: Package, badge: pendingCount },
    { name: 'تنظیمات', href: '/admin/settings', icon: Settings },
  ];

  const SidebarContent = () => (
    <>
      <div className="h-20 flex items-center gap-3 px-6 border-b border-line shrink-0">
        <Image src="/logo.png" alt="فاطمه بیوتی لند" width={36} height={36} className="rounded-full border border-line" />
        <h2 className="text-xl font-black text-ink">فاطمه بیوتی لند</h2>
        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-chalk border border-line text-[10px] font-bold rounded text-muted mt-1">مدیریت</span>
      </div>
      <nav className="p-4 space-y-1 flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
        {menuItems.map((item) => (
          <Link
            key={item.href}
            href={item.href}
            className={`flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 rounded-xl transition-all ${pathname === item.href ? 'bg-ink text-white shadow-md' : 'text-muted hover:bg-chalk hover:text-ink'}`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <item.icon className="w-5 h-5" />
              <span className="text-sm">{item.name}</span>
            </div>
            {item.badge && item.badge > 0 && (
              <span className="bg-sage text-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">{item.badge.toLocaleString('fa-IR')}</span>
            )}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </nav>
      <div className="p-4 border-t border-line shrink-0">
        <button onClick={handleLogout} className="flex items-center gap-3 w-full px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-rose hover:bg-rose/5 rounded-xl">
          <LogOut className="w-5 h-5" />
          <span>خروج</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </>
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[100dvh] max-w-[100vw] bg-chalk flex overflow-hidden">
      <Toaster position="top-center" richColors theme="light" />

      <aside className="w-64 bg-white border-l border-line hidden md:flex flex-col sticky top-0 h-screen z-20 shrink-0">
        <SidebarContent />
      </aside>

      <div className="md:hidden fixed top-0 left-0 right-0 h-16 bg-white border-b border-line flex items-center justify-between px-4 z-50">
        <button onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(true)} className="p-2 -mr-2 text-ink" aria-label="منو">
          <Menu size={24} />
        </button>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <h2 className="text-base font-black text-ink">فاطمه بیوتی لند</h2>
        </div>
        <Image src="/logo.png" alt="لوگو" width={32} height={32} className="rounded-full border border-line" />
      </div>

      {isMobileMenuOpen && (
        <>
          <div className="md:hidden fixed inset-0 bg-ink/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-[60]" onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)} />
          <div className="md:hidden fixed top-0 right-0 bottom-0 w-[280px] max-w-[85vw] bg-white z-[70] flex flex-col shadow-2xl">
            <div className="h-16 flex items-center justify-between px-4 border-b border-line shrink-0">
              <h2 className="font-black">منو</h2>
              <button onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)} className="p-2 -ml-2 text-muted">
                <X size={24} />
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
              <SidebarContent />
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      <main className="flex-1 min-h-0 min-w-0 flex flex-col">
        <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 min-w-0 overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden pt-16 md:pt-0 pb-20 md:pb-0">
          <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto p-4 md:p-8 lg:p-10">
            {children}
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      <AdminBottomNav pendingCount={pendingCount} />
    </div>
  );
}
===== END: src\app\admin\(dashboard)\admin-layout-client.tsx =====

===== FILE: src\app\admin\(dashboard)\articles\[id]\edit-form.tsx =====
'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { ArrowRight, Loader2, UploadCloud, Image as ImageIcon, X, Save, Eye, Sparkles, Settings, FileText, Globe, Wand2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import RichTextEditor from '@/components/admin/RichTextEditor';

export default function EditForm({ article }: { article: any }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [generating, setGenerating] = useState(false);
  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState(article.coverImage || '');
  
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ 
    title: article.title || '',
    slug: article.slug || '',
    excerpt: article.excerpt || '',
    content: article.content || '',
    category: article.category || 'education',
    status: article.status || 'draft',
    readTime: article.readTime || '۵ دقیقه',
    author: article.author || 'تیم فاطمه بیوتی لند',
    featured: article.featured || false,
    editorsPick: article.editorsPick || false,
    seoTitle: article.seoTitle || '',
    seoDescription: article.seoDescription || '',
  });

  // محاسبه خودکار زمان مطالعه
  useEffect(() => {
    const words = formData.content.trim().split(/\s+/).length;
    if (words > 10) {
      const minutes = Math.ceil(words / 200);
      setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, readTime: `${minutes.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} دقیقه` }));
    }
  }, [formData.content]);

  const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => {
    setFormData({ ...formData, [e.target.name]: e.target.value });
  };

  const handleToggle = (field: 'featured' | 'editorsPick') => {
    setFormData({ ...formData, [field]: !formData[field] });
  };

  const handleImageUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setUploading(true);
    const data = new FormData();
    data.append('file', file);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/upload', { method: 'POST', body: data });
      const result = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) { setImageUrl(result.url); toast.success('تصویر آپلود شد'); }
      else toast.error(result.message);
    } catch { toast.error('خطا در آپلود'); }
    finally { setUploading(false); }
  };

  const handleGenerateAI = async () => {
    setGenerating(true);
    const toastId = toast.loading('هوش مصنوعی در حال خلق تصویر است...');
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/articles/${article.id}/generate-cover`, {
        method: 'POST',
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        setImageUrl(data.coverImage);
        toast.success('تصویر با موفقیت خلق شد', { id: toastId });
      } else {
        toast.error(data.message || 'خطا در تولید تصویر', { id: toastId });
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error('خطا در ارتباط با سرور', { id: toastId });
    } finally {
      setGenerating(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    const payload = { ...formData, coverImage: imageUrl };

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/articles/${article.id}`, {
        method: 'PUT',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) { toast.success('مقاله ذخیره شد'); router.push('/admin/articles'); router.refresh(); }
      else toast.error(data.message);
    } catch { toast.error('خطا در ارتباط با سرور'); }
    finally { setLoading(false); }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="max-w-7xl mx-auto pb-20">
      <div className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-white border-b border-line py-3 px-4 md:px-6 flex items-center justify-between -mx-4 md:-mx-8 lg:-mx-10 mb-6 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Link href="/admin/articles" className="p-2 hover:bg-chalk rounded-lg transition-colors">
            <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5 text-ink" />
          </Link>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-lg md:text-xl font-bold text-ink line-clamp-1">{formData.title || 'ویرایش مقاله'}</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted">آخرین به‌روزرسانی: {new Date(article.updatedAt).toLocaleDateString('fa-IR')}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Link href={`/journal/${article.slug}`} target="_blank" className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-chalk text-ink text-xs font-bold rounded-lg border border-line hover:bg-white transition-colors">
            <Eye size={14} /> پیش‌نمایش
          </Link>
          <button type="submit" disabled={loading || uploading || generating} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2 bg-ink text-white text-xs md:text-sm font-bold rounded-lg shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-ink/90 transition-colors">
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> : <Save size={16} />}
            ذخیره مقاله
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          <div className="bg-white border border-line rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-5 md:p-6 space-y-5">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-bold text-ink flex items-center gap-2"><FileText size={16} className="text-sage"/> عنوان مقاله *</label>
              <input type="text" name="title" value={formData.title} onChange={handleChange} required className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-chalk border border-line rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sage/50 focus:bg-white transition-all text-lg font-bold text-ink" placeholder="عنوان جذاب مقاله را وارد کنید..." />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-bold text-ink">شناسه (Slug) *</label>
              <div className="flex items-center">
                <span className="bg-chalk border border-line border-l-0 rounded-r-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-muted font-mono" dir="ltr">/journal/</span>
                <input type="text" name="slug" value={formData.slug} onChange={handleChange} required dir="ltr" className="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 bg-chalk border border-line rounded-l-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sage/50 focus:bg-white transition-all font-mono text-sm" />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white border border-line rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-5 md:p-6 space-y-3">
            <label className="text-sm font-bold text-ink flex items-center gap-2"><Sparkles size={16} className="text-sage"/> خلاصه مقاله</label>
            <textarea name="excerpt" value={formData.excerpt} onChange={handleChange} rows={3} placeholder="یک خلاصه جذاب که در کارت مجله نمایش داده می‌شود..." className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-chalk border border-line rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sage/50 focus:bg-white transition-all text-sm leading-relaxed"></textarea>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white border border-line rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-5 md:p-6 space-y-3">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
              <label className="text-sm font-bold text-ink flex items-center gap-2"><FileText size={16} className="text-sage"/> محتوای اصلی *</label>
              <span className="text-xs text-muted">{formData.content.split(/\s+/).length.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} کلمه</span>
            </div>
            <RichTextEditor content={formData.content} onChange={(html) => setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, content: html }))} />
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white border border-line rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-5 md:p-6 space-y-4">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-ink flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-line pb-3"><Globe size={16} className="text-sage"/> تنظیمات سئو (SEO)</h3>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-xs font-medium text-muted">عنوان سئو (Meta Title)</label>
              <input type="text" name="seoTitle" value={formData.seoTitle} onChange={handleChange} placeholder="اگر خالی باشد، از عنوان مقاله استفاده می‌شود" className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-chalk border border-line rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sage/50 text-sm" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-xs font-medium text-muted">توضیحات سئو (Meta Description)</label>
              <textarea name="seoDescription" value={formData.seoDescription} onChange={handleChange} rows={2} placeholder="اگر خالی باشد، از خلاصه مقاله استفاده می‌شود" className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-chalk border border-line rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sage/50 text-sm"></textarea>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-6">
          <div className="bg-white border border-line rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-5 space-y-4">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-ink flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-line pb-3"><Settings size={16} className="text-sage"/> انتشار</h3>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-xs font-medium text-muted">وضعیت</label>
              <select name="status" value={formData.status} onChange={handleChange} className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-chalk border border-line rounded-lg cursor-pointer text-sm font-medium">
                <option value="draft">پیش‌نویس</option>
                <option value="published">منتشر شده</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <label className="text-xs font-medium text-muted">دسته‌بندی</label>
              <select name="category" value={formData.category} onChange={handleChange} className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-chalk border border-line rounded-lg cursor-pointer text-sm">
                <option value="education">آموزش</option>
                <option value="buying-guide">راهنمای خرید</option>
                <option value="skincare">مراقبت پوست</option>
                <option value="haircare">مراقبت مو</option>
                <option value="makeup">آرایشی</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label className="text-xs font-medium text-muted">نویسنده</label>
                <input type="text" name="author" value={formData.author} onChange={handleChange} className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-chalk border border-line rounded-lg text-xs" />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label className="text-xs font-medium text-muted">زمان مطالعه</label>
                <input type="text" name="readTime" value={formData.readTime} onChange={handleChange} className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-chalk border border-line rounded-lg text-xs" />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white border border-line rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-5 space-y-4">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-line pb-3">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-ink flex items-center gap-2"><ImageIcon size={16} className="text-sage"/> تصویر کاور</h3>
              <button type="button" onClick={handleGenerateAI} disabled={generating || uploading} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 to-rose-500 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg shadow-sm hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity disabled:opacity-50">
                {generating ? <Loader2 size={12} className="animate-spin" /> : <Wand2 size={12} />}
                {generating ? 'در حال خلق...' : 'تولید با AI'}
              </button>
            </div>
            
            <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-line rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-chalk relative min-h-[140px]">
              {imageUrl ? (
                <div className="relative w-full">
                  <div className="aspect-video rounded-lg overflow-hidden bg-white border border-line">
                    <img src={imageUrl} alt="Cover" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  </div>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => setImageUrl('')} className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-rose text-white p-1.5 rounded-md text-xs hover:bg-rose/90">
                    <X size={14} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <ImageIcon className="w-8 h-8 text-muted mb-2" />
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted mb-3 text-center">تصویر کاور را آپلود کنید یا از AI بگیرید</p>
                  <label className="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-line rounded-lg text-xs font-bold text-ink cursor-pointer hover:bg-chalk transition-colors">
                    <UploadCloud size={14} className="inline mr-1" /> انتخاب دستی
                    <input type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handleImageUpload} disabled={uploading} className="hidden" />
                  </label>
                </>
              )}
              {(uploading || generating) && <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-white/80 flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl"><Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 animate-spin text-sage" /></div>}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white border border-line rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-5 space-y-4">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-ink flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-line pb-3"><Sparkles size={16} className="text-sage"/> تنظیمات ویژه</h3>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => handleToggle('featured')} className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl border transition-all ${formData.featured ? 'bg-sage/10 border-sage text-sage' : 'bg-chalk border-line text-muted'}`}>
              <span className="text-sm font-medium">مقاله شاخص</span>
              <div className={`w-9 h-5 rounded-full relative transition-colors ${formData.featured ? 'bg-sage' : 'bg-line'}`}>
                <div className={`absolute top-0.5 w-4 h-4 bg-white rounded-full shadow transition-transform ${formData.featured ? 'left-0.5' : 'left-4.5'}`}></div>
              </div>
            </button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => handleToggle('editorsPick')} className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl border transition-all ${formData.editorsPick ? 'bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500 text-amber-600' : 'bg-chalk border-line text-muted'}`}>
              <span className="text-sm font-medium">انتخاب سردبیر</span>
              <div className={`w-9 h-5 rounded-full relative transition-colors ${formData.editorsPick ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-line'}`}>
                <div className={`absolute top-0.5 w-4 h-4 bg-white rounded-full shadow transition-transform ${formData.editorsPick ? 'left-0.5' : 'left-4.5'}`}></div>
              </div>
            </button>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
